### [116] [Impact of enriched meaning representations for language generation in dialogue tasks: A comprehensive exploration of the relevance of tasks, corpora and metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29518)
*Alain Vázquez,Maria Inés Torres*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在对话自然语言生成中，通过提供任务示例（MR-句子对）来增强生成模型性能的方法，发现该方法在复杂任务、小规模高变异性数据集及零样本场景下均有效，并指出基于语义的评估指标（尤其是基于人类评分训练的指标）能更准确地捕捉生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升对话系统中自然语言生成的多样性与准确性，探究如何利用任务示例信息改善生成结果。

Method: 引入一个从原始数据集中提取的MR-句子对作为任务示例，在训练和推理时丰富输入；在四个不同特征的数据集上使用五种语言相关指标进行分析。

Result: 增强后的输入在MR和句子高变异性的小型数据集以及零样本设置中表现更优；语义类指标比词汇类指标更能准确反映生成质量，特别是基于人类评分训练的指标能识别出嵌入式指标常忽略的语义遗漏等问题；Slot Accuracy和DA Accuracy结果优异，显示模型具有快速任务适应性和语义稳定性。

Conclusion: 为MR-to-text生成引入示范样本是有效的，尤其适用于复杂或数据稀缺场景；应优先采用基于人类判断的语义评估指标来更精准评价生成质量。

Abstract: Conversational systems should generate diverse language forms to interact fluently and accurately with users. In this context, Natural Language Generation (NLG) engines convert Meaning Representations (MRs) into sentences, directly influencing user perception. These MRs usually encode the communicative function (e.g., inform, request, confirm) via DAs and enumerate the semantic content with slot-value pairs. In this work, our objective is to analyse whether providing a task demonstrator to the generator enhances the generations of a fine-tuned model. This demonstrator is an MR-sentence pair extracted from the original dataset that enriches the input at training and inference time. The analysis involves five metrics that focus on different linguistic aspects, and four datasets that differ in multiple features, such as domain, size, lexicon, MR variability, and acquisition process. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study on dialogue NLG implementing a comparative analysis of the impact of MRs on generation quality across domains, corpus characteristics, and the metrics used to evaluate these generations. Our key insight is that the proposed enriched inputs are effective for complex tasks and small datasets with high variability in MRs and sentences. They are also beneficial in zero-shot settings for any domain. Moreover, the analysis of the metrics shows that semantic metrics capture generation quality more accurately than lexical metrics. In addition, among these semantic metrics, those trained with human ratings can detect omissions and other subtle semantic issues that embedding-based metrics often miss. Finally, the evolution of the metric scores and the excellent results for Slot Accuracy and Dialogue Act Accuracy demonstrate that the generative models present fast adaptability to different tasks and robustness at semantic and communicative intention levels.

</details>


### [117] [Baby Scale: Investigating Models Trained on Individual Children's Language Input](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29522)
*Steven Y. Feng,Alvin W. M. Tan,Michael C. Frank*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究利用儿童自然语言数据（BabyView数据集）训练语言模型，探讨了模型在小规模数据下的学习表现，并分析了数据质量对模型和儿童语言学习的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型需要远超儿童实际接触的语言数据量才能达到可用水平，研究旨在缩小这一‘数据差距’，理解语言知识如何从儿童真实语言环境中产生。

Method: 使用BabyView数据集（6-36个月儿童的视频转录文本）训练语言模型，评估其在语法、语义和世界知识任务上的扩展性能，分析不同儿童数据间的差异，并考察语言特征与模型及儿童学习结果的关系。

Result: 在儿童规模数据下，语言模型在语法任务上表现尚可，但在语义和世界知识任务上弱于合成数据训练的模型；不同儿童的数据导致模型表现差异显著；模型性能与输入语言的分布性和互动性特征密切相关；模型对单词的预测概率与儿童对该词的学习程度相关。

Conclusion: 语言输入的质量（如分布性和互动性特征）不仅影响语言模型的小样本学习效率，也可能反映人类语言习得的关键机制，为构建高效小规模语言模型和理解儿童语言发展提供启示。

Abstract: Modern language models (LMs) must be trained on many orders of magnitude more words of training data than human children receive before they begin to produce useful behavior. Assessing the nature and origins of this "data gap" requires benchmarking LMs on human-scale datasets to understand how linguistic knowledge emerges from children's natural training data. Using transcripts from the BabyView dataset (videos from children ages 6-36 months), we investigate (1) scaling performance at child-scale data regimes, (2) variability in model performance across datasets from different children's experiences and linguistic predictors of dataset quality, and (3) relationships between model and child language learning outcomes. LMs trained on child data show acceptable scaling for grammar tasks, but lower scaling on semantic and world knowledge tasks than models trained on synthetic data; we also observe substantial variability on data from different children. Beyond dataset size, performance is most associated with a combination of distributional and interactional linguistic features, broadly consistent with what makes high-quality input for child language development. Finally, model likelihoods for individual words correlate with children's learning of those words, suggesting that properties of child-directed input may influence both model learning and human language development. Overall, understanding what properties make language data efficient for learning can enable more powerful small-scale language models while also shedding light on human language acquisition.

</details>


### [118] [Can LLM Agents Identify Spoken Dialects like a Linguist?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29541)
*Tobias Bystrich,Lukas Hamm,Maria Hassan,Lea Fischbach,Lucie Flek,Akbar Karimi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了大型语言模型（LLM）在瑞士德语方言分类中的应用，利用ASR生成的音素转录结合语言学资源提升LLM性能，并提供了LLM与人类语言学家的基线对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于方言语音标注数据稀缺，音频方言分类任务具有挑战性，特别是对于瑞士德语等语言，因此探索LLM在此类任务中的潜力具有重要意义。

Method: 使用ASR系统生成的音素转录，并结合方言特征图、元音历史和语言规则等语言学资源，将这些信息输入LLM进行方言分类，同时建立LLM和人类语言学家的基线模型。

Result: 实验结果表明，在提供语言学信息的情况下，LLM的预测性能得到提升；人类基线显示自动生成的转录对分类有帮助，但仍有改进空间。

Conclusion: 结合语言学知识可以增强LLM在方言分类中的表现，未来可进一步优化自动转录质量和融合策略以提升分类效果。

Abstract: Due to the scarcity of labeled dialectal speech, audio dialect classification is a challenging task for most languages, including Swiss German. In this work, we explore the ability of large language models (LLMs) as agents in understanding the dialects and whether they can show comparable performance to models such as HuBERT in dialect classification. In addition, we provide an LLM baseline and a human linguist one. Our approach uses phonetic transcriptions produced by ASR systems and combines them with linguistic resources such as dialect feature maps, vowel history, and rules. Our findings indicate that, when linguistic information is provided, the LLM predictions improve. The human baseline shows that automatically generated transcriptions can be beneficial for such classifications, but also presents opportunities for improvement.

</details>


### [119] [Bringing Up a Bilingual BabyLM: Investigating Multilingual Language Acquisition Using Small-Scale Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29552)
*Linda Zeng,Steven Y. Feng,Michael C. Frank*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过使用语言模型训练模拟多语言输入条件，研究发现双语模型在两种语言中的表现与单语模型相当，表明双语输入对统计学习并无本质挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨儿童同时学习多种语言时是否存在学习延迟或输入结构优劣的问题，由于现实中难以随机分配儿童为多语者且数据匹配困难，因此需要一种可控的模拟方法。

Method: 利用GPT-2模型在合成数据和机器翻译生成的1亿词级单语和双语数据集上进行训练，模拟多种双语输入环境，并从困惑度、语法性和语义知识方面评估模型表现。

Result: 在不同模型规模和评估指标下，双语模型在一种语言上的表现与单语模型相似，同时在第二语言上也表现出较强能力。

Conclusion: 不同的双语输入方式之间没有显著差异，双语输入本身并不构成对无偏好统计学习者的根本性挑战。

Abstract: Multilingualism is incredibly common around the world, leading to many important theoretical and practical questions about how children learn multiple languages at once. For example, does multilingual acquisition lead to delays in learning? Are there better and worse ways to structure multilingual input? Many correlational studies address these questions, but it is surprisingly difficult to get definitive answers because children cannot be randomly assigned to be multilingual and data are typically not matched between languages. We use language model training as a method for simulating a variety of highly controlled exposure conditions, and create matched 100M-word mono- and bilingual datasets using synthetic data and machine translation. We train GPT-2 models on monolingual and bilingual data organized to reflect a range of exposure regimes, and evaluate their performance on perplexity, grammaticality, and semantic knowledge. Across model scales and measures, bilingual models perform similarly to monolingual models in one language, but show strong performance in the second language as well. These results suggest that there are no strong differences between different bilingual exposure regimes, and that bilingual input poses no in-principle challenges for agnostic statistical learners.

</details>


### [120] [When Can We Trust LLM Graders? Calibrating Confidence for Automated Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29559)
*Robinson Ferrer,Damla Turgut,Zhongzhou Chen,Shashank Sonkar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了如何预测大语言模型（LLM）在自动评分中是否可能正确，比较了三种置信度估计方法，发现自报告置信度在校准性能上表现最佳且成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLMs在自动评分中有潜力，但其输出不可靠；因此需要一种方法来预测何时其评分是可信的，以实现选择性自动化。

Method: 比较了三种置信度估计方法：自报告置信度、自一致性投票和标记概率，并在七个不同规模的LLM上进行实验，涵盖三个教育数据集。

Result: 自报告置信度在校准误差（ECE）方面表现最好（平均0.166），优于自一致性（0.229），且后者推理成本高5倍；更大的模型校准更好，GPT-OSS-120B表现最佳（平均ECE 0.100，AUC 0.668）；置信度普遍呈顶部偏斜，存在‘置信度下限’。

Conclusion: 简单地让LLM报告其置信度是一种识别可靠评分预测的实用方法，尤其适合高置信预测自动处理、低置信情况交由人工复核的场景。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise for automated grading, but their outputs can be unreliable. Rather than improving grading accuracy directly, we address a complementary problem: \textit{predicting when an LLM grader is likely to be correct}. This enables selective automation where high-confidence predictions are processed automatically while uncertain cases are flagged for human review. We compare three confidence estimation methods (self-reported confidence, self-consistency voting, and token probability) across seven LLMs of varying scale (4B to 120B parameters) on three educational datasets: RiceChem (long-answer chemistry), SciEntsBank, and Beetle (short-answer science). Our experiments reveal that self-reported confidence consistently achieves the best calibration across all conditions (avg ECE 0.166 vs 0.229 for self-consistency). Surprisingly, self-consistency remains 38\% worse despite requiring 5$\times$ the inference cost. Larger models exhibit substantially better calibration though gains vary by dataset and method (e.g., a 28\% ECE reduction for self-reported), with GPT-OSS-120B achieving the best calibration (avg ECE 0.100) and strong discrimination (avg AUC 0.668). We also observe that confidence is strongly top-skewed across methods, creating a ``confidence floor'' that practitioners must account for when setting thresholds. These findings suggest that simply asking LLMs to report their confidence provides a practical approach for identifying reliable grading predictions. Code is available \href{https://github.com/sonkar-lab/llm_grading_calibration}{here}.

</details>


### [121] [Learning Diagnostic Reasoning for Decision Support in Toxicology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29608)
*Nico Oberländer,David Bani-Harouni,Tobias Zellner,Nassir Navab,Florian Eyer,Matthias Keicher*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DeToxR，首个将强化学习应用于急诊中毒诊断的决策支持模型，通过融合非结构化叙述和结构化医疗数据，显著优于基线模型和专家毒理学家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在急性多物质中毒场景中，临床医生面临信息不全和症状非特异性的挑战，现有大语言模型在此类高风险环境下的表现不佳，需提升其推理能力以支持关键决策。

Method: 提出DeToxR模型，采用基于组相对策略优化（GRPO）的强化学习方法，构建鲁棒的数据融合引擎，利用多标签一致性的临床绩效奖励信号优化LLM的推理过程，实现对14类毒物的多标签预测。

Result: 该模型显著优于未适配的基础LLM和监督式基线模型，并在临床验证研究中超越专家毒理学家（Micro-F1: 0.644 vs. 0.473），能更准确识别共摄入毒物并减少误报。

Conclusion: 经强化学习对齐的大语言模型在高风险医疗决策中具有潜力，可有效整合非结构化现场描述与结构化医学数据，提升中毒诊断准确性。

Abstract: Acute poly-substance intoxication requires rapid, life-saving decisions under substantial uncertainty, as clinicians must rely on incomplete ingestion details and nonspecific symptoms. Effective diagnostic reasoning in this chaotic environment requires fusing unstructured, non-medical narratives (e.g. paramedic scene descriptions and unreliable patient self-reports or known histories), with structured medical data like vital signs. While Large Language Models (LLMs) show potential for processing such heterogeneous inputs, they struggle in this setting, often underperforming simple baselines that rely solely on patient histories. To address this, we present DeToxR (Decision-support for Toxicology with Reasoning), the first adaptation of Reinforcement Learning (RL) to emergency toxicology. We design a robust data-fusion engine for multi-label prediction across 14 substance classes based on an LLM finetuned with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). We optimize the model's reasoning directly using a clinical performance reward. By formulating a multi-label agreement metric as the reward signal, the model is explicitly penalized for missing co-ingested substances and hallucinating absent poisons. Our model significantly outperforms its unadapted base LLM counterpart and supervised baselines. Furthermore, in a clinical validation study, the model indicates a clinical advantage by outperforming an expert toxicologist in identifying the correct poisons (Micro-F1: 0.644 vs. 0.473). These results demonstrate the potential of RL-aligned LLMs to synthesize unstructured pre-clinical narratives and structured medical data for decision support in high-stakes environments.

</details>


### [122] [Agenda-based Narrative Extraction: Steering Pathfinding Algorithms with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29661)
*Brian Felipe Keith-Norambuena,Carolina Inés Rojas-Córdova,Claudio Juvenal Meneses-Villegas,Elizabeth Johanna Lam-Esquenazi,Angélica María Flores-Bustos,Ignacio Alejandro Molina-Villablanca,Joshua Emanuel Leyton-Vallejos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于议程的叙事提取方法，通过将大语言模型（LLM）引入路径发现过程，实现在保持叙事连贯性的同时支持用户指定视角和多故事线生成。实验表明该方法在议程对齐上优于关键词匹配，且对连贯性影响较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有叙事提取方法在连贯性、交互性和多故事线支持之间存在权衡，难以同时满足用户对不同视角引导和高质量路径的需求。

Method: 将大语言模型集成到Narrative Trails的路径寻找过程中，在每一步使用LLM根据给定议程对候选文档进行排序，从而引导故事线构建；通过更换不同议程生成不同的叙事路径。

Result: 在新闻语料库上的评估显示，LLM驱动的引导比关键词匹配在语义议程上实现9.9%更高的对齐度（p=0.017），在“政权镇压”议程上提升达13.3%（p=0.037）；而连贯性仅下降2.2%。对抗性议程评分均低，说明无法捏造不支持的叙事。

Conclusion: 基于议程的叙事提取方法成功平衡了叙事连贯性与用户引导需求，支持多视角故事生成，且不会偏离原始材料，为交互式叙事分析提供了有效新途径。

Abstract: Existing narrative extraction methods face a trade-off between coherence, interactivity, and multi-storyline support. Narrative Maps supports rich interaction and generates multiple storylines as a byproduct of its coverage constraints, though this comes at the cost of individual path coherence. Narrative Trails achieves high coherence through maximum capacity path optimization but provides no mechanism for user guidance or multiple perspectives. We introduce agenda-based narrative extraction, a method that bridges this gap by integrating large language models into the Narrative Trails pathfinding process to steer storyline construction toward user-specified perspectives. Our approach uses an LLM at each step to rank candidate documents based on their alignment with a given agenda while maintaining narrative coherence. Running the algorithm with different agendas yields different storylines through the same corpus. We evaluated our approach on a news article corpus using LLM judges with Claude Opus 4.5 and GPT 5.1, measuring both coherence and agenda alignment across 64 endpoint pairs and 6 agendas. LLM-driven steering achieves 9.9% higher alignment than keyword matching on semantic agendas (p=0.017), with 13.3% improvement on \textit{Regime Crackdown} specifically (p=0.037), while keyword matching remains competitive on agendas with literal keyword overlap. The coherence cost is minimal: LLM steering reduces coherence by only 2.2% compared to the agenda-agnostic baseline. Counter-agendas that contradict the source material score uniformly low (2.2-2.5) across all methods, confirming that steering cannot fabricate unsupported narratives.

</details>


### [123] [ENEIDE: A High Quality Silver Standard Dataset for Named Entity Recognition and Linking in Historical Italian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29801)
*Cristian Santini,Sebastian Barzaghi,Paolo Sernani,Emanuele Frontoni,Laura Melosi,Mehwish Alam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ENIEDE是首个面向历史意大利语文本的多领域公开命名实体识别与链接（NERL）数据集，包含来自两位重要历史人物数字文集的2,111份文档和8,000多个实体标注，支持时间性实体消歧与跨域评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏适用于历史意大利语的高质量、公开可用的命名实体识别与链接（NERL）数据集，现有资源难以支持跨域和时序性分析需求。

Method: 从经过人工校对的学术数字版本（Digital Zibaldone 和 Aldo Moro Digitale）中半自动提取命名实体，结合质量控制与标注增强流程，生成带有Wikidata链接的多类型实体标注，并划分训练、开发与测试集。

Result: 构建了包含2,111份文档和超过8,000个实体标注的ENIEDE数据集，覆盖人物、地点、组织、文学作品等类型，包含无法映射到知识图谱的NIL实体；实验显示当前最先进模型在该数据集上仍有显著性能差距，尤其是零样本方法表现有限。

Conclusion: ENIEDE填补了历史意大利语NERL资源的空白，为跨域、跨时代命名实体研究提供了基准数据，推动低资源历史语言处理的发展。

Abstract: This paper introduces ENEIDE (Extracting Named Entities from Italian Digital Editions), a silver standard dataset for Named Entity Recognition and Linking (NERL) in historical Italian texts. The corpus comprises 2,111 documents with over 8,000 entity annotations semi-automatically extracted from two scholarly digital editions: Digital Zibaldone, the philosophical diary of the Italian poet Giacomo Leopardi (1798--1837), and Aldo Moro Digitale, the complete works of the Italian politician Aldo Moro (1916--1978). Annotations cover multiple entity types (person, location, organization, literary work) linked to Wikidata identifiers, including NIL entities that cannot be mapped to the knowledge graph. To the best of our knowledge, ENEIDE represents the first multi-domain, publicly available NERL dataset for historical Italian with training, development, and test splits. We present a methodology for semi-automatic annotations extraction from manually curated scholarly digital editions, including quality control and annotation enhancement procedures. Baseline experiments using state-of-the-art models demonstrate the dataset's challenge for NERL and the gap between zero-shot approaches and fine-tuned models. The dataset's diachronic coverage spanning two centuries makes it particularly suitable for temporal entity disambiguation and cross-domain evaluation. ENEIDE is released under a CC BY-NC-SA 4.0 license.

</details>


### [124] [SNEAK: Evaluating Strategic Communication and Information Leakage in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29846)
*Adar Avsian,Larry Heck*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SNEAK是一个新基准，用于评估大语言模型在不对称信息下的选择性信息共享能力，通过衡量消息对盟友的效用和对对手的信息泄露来评估模型在保密与沟通之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型基准主要关注推理、知识或指令遵循，缺乏对多智能体环境中战略性沟通（特别是在需隐藏敏感信息时）的评估。因此需要构建专门衡量信息选择性披露能力的基准。

Method: 提出SNEAK基准：给定语义类别、候选词和秘密词，要求模型生成一条暗示秘密但不明显泄露的消息；使用两个模拟智能体（盟友和变色龙）分别评估消息的效用（是否被正确理解）和泄露程度（是否被推断出秘密）。

Result: 实验表明当前语言模型在平衡信息效用与保密性方面表现不佳，存在明显的信息泄露或沟通不足问题；相比之下，人类参与者表现远超模型，最高得分达到模型的四倍。

Conclusion: 战略性沟通在信息不对称场景下仍是当前语言模型的重大挑战，SNEAK为评估此类能力提供了有效框架，并揭示了人机性能间的显著差距。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in multi-agent settings where communication must balance informativeness and secrecy. In such settings, an agent may need to signal information to collaborators while preventing an adversary from inferring sensitive details. However, existing LLM benchmarks primarily evaluate capabilities such as reasoning, factual knowledge, or instruction following, and do not directly measure strategic communication under asymmetric information. We introduce SNEAK (Secret-aware Natural language Evaluation for Adversarial Knowledge), a benchmark for evaluating selective information sharing in language models. In SNEAK, a model is given a semantic category, a candidate set of words, and a secret word, and must generate a message that indicates knowledge of the secret without revealing it too clearly. We evaluate generated messages using two simulated agents with different information states: an ally, who knows the secret and must identify the intended message, and a chameleon, who does not know the secret and attempts to infer it from the message. This yields two complementary metrics: utility, measuring how well the message communicates to collaborators, and leakage, measuring how much information it reveals to an adversary. Using this framework, we analyze the trade-off between informativeness and secrecy in modern language models and show that strategic communication under asymmetric information remains a challenging capability for current systems. Notably, human participants outperform all evaluated models by a large margin, achieving up to four times higher scores.

</details>


### [125] [Towards Empowering Consumers through Sentence-level Readability Scoring in German ESG Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29861)
*Benjamin Josef Schüßler,Jakob Prange*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究扩展了一个德语ESG报告的句子级数据集，并通过众包获取可读性标注，评估多种可读性评分方法与人类判断的相关性，发现微调的小型Transformer模型在预测人类可读性方面表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着可持续发展信息的快速增长，消费者需要可靠且易于理解的ESG报告内容，但这些报告是否真正易于非专业读者理解仍不明确，因此需评估其实际可读性。

Method: 扩展了现有的德语ESG报告句子数据集，加入众包方式收集的可读性标注，并应用多种可读性评分方法（包括大语言模型提示和微调Transformer模型），比较其预测误差与人类排序的相关性。

Result: 研究表明，母语者普遍认为ESG报告中的句子较易理解，但可读性具有主观性；尽管大模型提示有一定区分能力，微调的小型Transformer模型预测人类可读性的误差最小，多模型平均可略微提升性能但牺牲推理速度。

Conclusion: 当前ESG报告整体可读性尚可，但仍有改进空间；基于微调的小型模型是预测人类可读性的最有效方法，为未来自动评估和优化报告语言提供了可行路径。

Abstract: With the ever-growing urgency of sustainability in the economy and society, and the massive stream of information that comes with it, consumers need reliable access to that information. To address this need, companies began publishing so called Environmental, Social, and Governance (ESG) reports, both voluntarily and forced by law. To serve the public, these reports must be addressed not only to financial experts but also to non-expert audiences. But are they written clearly enough? In this work, we extend an existing sentence-level dataset of German ESG reports with crowdsourced readability annotations. We find that, in general, native speakers perceive sentences in ESG reports as easy to read, but also that readability is subjective. We apply various readability scoring methods and evaluate them regarding their prediction error and correlation with human rankings. Our analysis shows that, while LLM prompting has potential for distinguishing clear from hard-to-read sentences, a small finetuned transformer predicts human readability with the lowest error. Averaging predictions of multiple models can slightly improve the performance at the cost of slower inference.

</details>


### [126] [FLEURS-Kobani: Extending the FLEURS Dataset for Northern Kurdish](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29892)
*Daban Q. Jaff,Mohammad Mohammadamini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了FLEURS-Kobani，这是FLEURS基准的北库尔德语（KMR）口语扩展版本，包含5,162个经过验证的语句，总计18小时24分钟，由31名母语者录制。该数据集为低资源语言北库尔德语提供了首个公开的自动语音识别（ASR）和语音到文本翻译（S2TT）评估基准，并在研究中公开发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于FLEURS数据集未包含北库尔德语，限制了该语言在自动语音识别和语音翻译任务中的基准测试，因此需要构建一个针对北库尔德语的高质量、公开可用的语音数据集以支持相关研究。

Method: 通过扩展FLEURS基准，收集并验证北库尔德语语音数据，构建FLEURS-Kobani数据集；采用Whisper v3-large模型进行ASR和端到端S2TT任务的微调，并尝试两阶段微调策略（先在Common Voice上微调，再在FLEURS-Kobani上微调）以提升性能。

Result: FLEURS-Kobani数据集包含5,162个有效语句，总时长18小时24分钟，来自31名母语 speaker；在ASR任务中，两阶段微调策略取得最佳效果（测试集WER为28.11，CER为9.84）；在端到端S2TT（KMR→EN）任务中，Whisper模型达到8.68 BLEU；同时提供了基于枢纽语言的目标参考和级联S2TT设置的结果。

Conclusion: FLEURS-Kobani填补了北库尔德语在多语言语音基准中的空白，为ASR、S2TT和S2ST任务提供了首个公开可用的评估资源，有助于推动低资源语言语音技术的发展。

Abstract: FLEURS offers n-way parallel speech for 100+ languages, but Northern Kurdish is not one of them, which limits benchmarking for automatic speech recognition and speech translation tasks in this language. We present FLEURS-Kobani, a Northern Kurdish (ISO 639-3 KMR) spoken extension of the FLEURS benchmark. The FLEURS-Kobani dataset consists of 5,162 validated utterances, totaling 18 hours and 24 minutes. The data were recorded by 31 native speakers. It extends benchmark coverage to an under-resourced Kurdish variety. As baselines, we fine-tuned Whisper v3-large for ASR and E2E S2TT. A two-stage fine-tuning strategy (Common Voice to FLEURS-Kobani) yields the best ASR performance (WER 28.11, CER 9.84 on test). For E2E S2TT (KMR to EN), Whisper achieves 8.68 BLEU on test; we additionally report pivot-derived targets and a cascaded S2TT setup. FLEURS-Kobani provides the first public Northern Kurdish benchmark for evaluation of ASR, S2TT and S2ST tasks. The dataset is publicly released for research use under a CC BY 4.0 license.

</details>


### [127] [Rewrite the News: Tracing Editorial Reuse Across News Agencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29937)
*Soveatin Kuntur,Nina Smirnova,Anna Wroblewska,Philipp Mayr,Sebastijan Razboršek Maček*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种弱监督方法，用于检测多语言新闻报道中的句子级文本复用，无需完全翻译即可识别跨语言复用，并利用发布时间确定来源。分析显示复用内容多出现在文章中后部，且以非字面改写为主。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应对多语言新闻环境中信息过载问题，帮助记者自动筛选重复内容，提升新闻编辑效率，并弥补以往单语复用研究的局限。

Method: 采用弱监督方法进行跨语言句子级复用检测，结合发布时序筛选最早可能来源，对斯洛文尼亚通讯社（STA）的英文文章与15家外联社在七种语言下的报道进行对比分析。

Result: 分析了1,037篇STA文章和237,551篇外联社文章，识别出1,087对对齐的句子对；52%的STA文章包含复用内容，而外联社文章为1.6%；复用主要为非字面形式，常融合多个来源；复用内容多位于文章中后部，导语部分更倾向原创。

Conclusion: 跨语言文本复用广泛存在且具有结构性特征，仅靠词汇匹配会遗漏大量编辑层面的复用行为；所提方法可有效支持多语言新闻中的自动化复用检测与来源追溯。

Abstract: This paper investigates sentence-level text reuse in multilingual journalism, analyzing where reused content occurs within articles. We present a weakly supervised method for detecting sentence-level cross-lingual reuse without requiring full translations, designed to support automated pre-selection to reduce information overload for journalists (Holyst et al., 2024). The study compares English-language articles from the Slovenian Press Agency (STA) with reports from 15 foreign agencies (FA) in seven languages, using publication timestamps to retain the earliest likely foreign source for each reused sentence. We analyze 1,037 STA and 237,551 FA articles from two time windows (October 7-November 2, 2023; February 1-28, 2025) and identify 1,087 aligned sentence pairs after filtering to the earliest sources. Reuse occurs in 52% of STA articles and 1.6% of FA articles and is predominantly non-literal, involving paraphrase and compositional reuse from multiple sources. Reused content tends to appear in the middle and end of English articles, while leads are more often original, indicating that simple lexical matching overlooks substantial editorial reuse. Compared with prior work focused on monolingual overlap, we (i) detect reuse across languages without requiring full translation, (ii) use publication timing to identify likely sources, and (iii) analyze where reused material is situated within articles. Dataset and code: https://github.com/kunturs/lrec2026-rewrite-news.

</details>


### [128] [Structural Feature Engineering for Generative Engine Optimization: How Content Structure Shapes Citation Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29979)
*Junwei Yu,Mufeng Yang,Yepeng Ding,Hiroyuki Sato*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了GEO-SFE框架，系统研究了在生成式搜索引擎优化中结构特征对内容引用率的影响，涵盖宏观、中观和微观三个层次的结构设计，并通过实验验证了其在多个生成式引擎中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI驱动的搜索引擘从传统的链接检索转向直接生成答案并选择性引用来源，现有生成式引擎优化（GEO）方法主要关注语义内容调整，而结构特征对引用行为的影响尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出GEO-SFE框架，将内容结构分解为宏观结构（文档架构）、中观结构（信息分块）和微观结构（视觉强调）三个层次，建模其对不同生成式引擎引用概率的影响，并开发兼顾语义完整性和结构有效性的优化策略与预测模型。

Result: 在六个主流生成式引擎上的实验表明，该方法使引用率平均提升17.3%，主观质量提升18.5%，展现出良好的通用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 结构优化是生成式引擎优化（GEO）的基础组成部分，本研究为提升大语言模型环境下内容可见性提供了数据驱动的方法论支持。

Abstract: The proliferation of AI-powered search engines has shifted information discovery from traditional link-based retrieval to direct answer generation with selective source citation, creating new challenges for content visibility. While existing Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) approaches focus primarily on semantic content modification, the role of structural features in influencing citation behavior remains underexplored.
  In this paper, we propose GEO-SFE, a systematic framework for structural feature engineering in generative engine optimization. Our approach decomposes content structure into three hierarchical levels: macro-structure (document architecture), meso-structure (information chunking), and micro-structure (visual emphasis), and models their impact on citation probability across different generative engine architectures. We develop architecture-aware optimization strategies and predictive models that preserve semantic integrity while improving structural effectiveness.
  Experimental evaluation across six mainstream generative engines demonstrates consistent improvements in citation rate (17.3 percent) and subjective quality (18.5 percent), validating the effectiveness and generalizability of the proposed framework. This work establishes structural optimization as a foundational component of GEO, providing a data-driven methodology for enhancing content visibility in LLM-powered information ecosystems.

</details>


### [129] [Enhancing Structural Mapping with LLM-derived Abstractions for Analogical Reasoning in Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29997)
*Mohammadhossein Khojasteh,Yifan Jiang,Stefano De Giorgis,Frank van Harmelen,Filip Ilievski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为YARN的模块化框架，利用大语言模型（LLM）对叙事进行分解和抽象，并通过结构映射提升机器在叙事类比推理中的表现，实验表明抽象能显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认知引擎难以直接应用于叙事结构之间的类比推理，且大语言模型的表现受提示格式和表面相似性影响较大，因此需要探索如何通过LLM生成的抽象来增强结构映射能力。

Method: 提出了YARN框架，将叙事分解为单元并进行四层抽象，然后通过映射组件对不同故事中的元素进行对齐以实现类比推理。

Result: 实验结果显示，引入抽象后模型性能一致提升，达到或优于端到端LLM基线；错误分析揭示了当前在抽象层次、隐式因果建模和类比模式分类上的挑战。

Conclusion: 结构化抽象有助于提升叙事中的类比推理能力，YARN框架支持对各组件贡献的系统分析，并为未来研究提供了可公开访问的代码。

Abstract: Analogical reasoning is a key driver of human generalization in problem-solving and argumentation. Yet, analogies between narrative structures remain challenging for machines. Cognitive engines for structural mapping are not directly applicable, as they assume pre-extracted entities, whereas LLMs' performance is sensitive to prompt format and the degree of surface similarity between narratives. This gap motivates a key question: What is the impact of enhancing structural mapping with LLM-derived abstractions on their analogical reasoning ability in narratives? To that end, we propose a modular framework named YARN (Yielding Abstractions for Reasoning in Narratives), which uses LLMs to decompose narratives into units, abstract these units, and then passes them to a mapping component that aligns elements across stories to perform analogical reasoning. We define and operationalize four levels of abstraction that capture both the general meaning of units and their roles in the story, grounded in prior work on framing. Our experiments reveal that abstractions consistently improve model performance, resulting in competitive or better performance than end-to-end LLM baselines. Closer error analysis reveals the remaining challenges in abstraction at the right level, in incorporating implicit causality, and an emerging categorization of analogical patterns in narratives. YARN enables systematic variation of experimental settings to analyze component contributions, and to support future work, we make the code for YARN openly available.

</details>


### [130] [ContextClaim: A Context-Driven Paradigm for Verifiable Claim Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.30025)
*Yufeng Li,Rrubaa Panchendrarajan,Arkaitz Zubiaga*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的可验证性声明检测方法ContextClaim，通过从声明中提取实体并从维基百科检索相关信息来增强上下文，利用大语言模型生成上下文摘要以辅助分类，从而提升不同数据集和模型设置下的检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的声明检测方法仅依赖于声明文本本身，难以判断其是否可验证，尤其是在需要外部证据支持的情况下。因此，引入上下文信息有助于更准确地识别可验证的声明。

Method: 提出ContextClaim框架：首先从输入声明中提取实体提及，然后从维基百科等结构化知识源检索相关信息，并使用大语言模型生成简洁的上下文摘要，供下游分类模型使用。在多种模型架构和学习设置下进行评估。

Result: 在CheckThat! 2022 COVID-19 Twitter数据集和PoliClaim政治辩论数据集上的实验表明，上下文增强能够提升可验证声明检测性能，但效果因领域、模型架构和学习设置而异。

Conclusion: 将证据检索提前到检测阶段是可行且有益的，ContextClaim为可验证声明检测提供了一个新的范式，尤其在结合大语言模型与外部知识时表现出潜力。

Abstract: Verifiable claim detection asks whether a claim expresses a factual statement that can, in principle, be assessed against external evidence. As an early filtering stage in automated fact-checking, it plays an important role in reducing the burden on downstream verification components. However, existing approaches to claim detection, whether based on check-worthiness or verifiability, rely solely on the claim text itself. This is a notable limitation for verifiable claim detection in particular, where determining whether a claim is checkable may benefit from knowing what entities and events it refers to and whether relevant information exists to support verification. Inspired by the established role of evidence retrieval in later-stage claim verification, we propose Context-Driven Claim Detection (ContextClaim), a paradigm that advances retrieval to the detection stage. ContextClaim extracts entity mentions from the input claim, retrieves relevant information from Wikipedia as a structured knowledge source, and employs large language models to produce concise contextual summaries for downstream classification. We evaluate ContextClaim on two datasets covering different topics and text genres, the CheckThat! 2022 COVID-19 Twitter dataset and the PoliClaim political debate dataset, across encoder-only and decoder-only models under fine-tuning, zero-shot, and few-shot settings. Results show that context augmentation can improve verifiable claim detection, although its effectiveness varies across domains, model architectures, and learning settings. Through component analysis, human evaluation, and error analysis, we further examine when and why the retrieved context contributes to more reliable verifiability judgments.

</details>


### [131] [Covertly improving intelligibility with data-driven adaptations of speech timing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.30032)
*Paige Tuttösí,Angelica Lim,H. Henny Yeung,Yue Wang,Jean-Julien Aucouturier*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究利用机器生成语音的进展，系统地探讨了有针对性的语速调整如何提高语言理解能力，发现特定时间模式的语速变化（如剪刀式模式）能显著提升母语和非母语听者的语音可懂度，且这种改进常未被察觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类说话者常通过整体放慢语速来帮助听力困难或非母语听众理解语言，但这种方法是否真正有效尚不清楚。因此，研究旨在探索更精确、有针对性的语速调整是否比全局放慢更有效，并揭示其对不同语言背景听者的影响。

Method: 采用反向相关实验确定语音速率在目标元音对比前的时间影响模式，并开发一种数据驱动的文本到语音算法，在新语音序列中复制该时间结构，通过多种句子和元音对比测试其效果。

Result: 发现语速的影响呈现稳定的‘剪刀式’时间模式，早期与晚期上下文窗口作用相反；该模式不仅促进非母语听者对目标元音的理解，母语听者在嘈杂条件下也依赖此模式；新算法生成的针对性放慢语音提升了词汇理解，但听者通常未意识到这一点，反而误认为整体放慢更清晰，尽管其实际增加了理解错误。

Conclusion: 有针对性的语速调整比全局放慢更能有效提升语音可懂度，且常不被听者察觉；研究提供了一种数据驱动的方法来优化机器生成语音的可访问性，可推广至其他语音理解和多样化听者场景。

Abstract: Human talkers often address listeners with language-comprehension challenges, such as hard-of-hearing or non-native adults, by globally slowing down their speech. However, it remains unclear whether this strategy actually makes speech more intelligible. Here, we take advantage of recent advancements in machine-generated speech allowing more precise control of speech rate in order to systematically examine how targeted speech-rate adjustments may improve comprehension. We first use reverse-correlation experiments to show that the temporal influence of speech rate prior to a target vowel contrast (ex. the tense-lax distinction) in fact manifests in a scissor-like pattern, with opposite effects in early versus late context windows; this pattern is remarkably stable both within individuals and across native L1-English listeners and L2-English listeners with French, Mandarin, and Japanese L1s. Second, we show that this speech rate structure not only facilitates L2 listeners' comprehension of the target vowel contrast, but that native listeners also rely on this pattern in challenging acoustic conditions. Finally, we build a data-driven text-to-speech algorithm that replicates this temporal structure on novel speech sequences. Across a variety of sentences and vowel contrasts, listeners remained unaware that such targeted slowing improved word comprehension. Strikingly, participants instead judged the common strategy of global slowing as clearer, even though it actually increased comprehension errors. Together, these results show that targeted adjustments to speech rate significantly aid intelligibility under challenging conditions, while often going unnoticed. More generally, this paper provides a data-driven methodology to improve the accessibility of machine-generated speech which can be extended to other aspects of speech comprehension and a wide variety of listeners and environments.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [A Semantic Observer Layer for Autonomous Vehicles: Pre-Deployment Feasibility Study of VLMs for Low-Latency Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28888)
*Kunal Runwal,Swaraj Gajare,Daniel Adejumo,Omkar Ankalkope,Siddhant Baroth,Aliasghar Arab*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于量化视觉-语言模型的语义观察层，用于自动驾驶中检测语义异常，实现实时性与安全性的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义异常是像素级检测器无法识别的安全隐患，对自动驾驶构成重大风险，需引入高层语义理解机制进行补充监测。

Method: 设计一个运行在1-2Hz的量化视觉-语言模型（VLM）作为语义观察层，集成于主控系统旁，使用Nvidia Cosmos-Reason1-7B模型，采用NVFP4量化和FlashAttention2优化推理速度。

Result: 相比未优化的FP16基线，推理时间缩短至约500ms，实现约50倍加速；发现NF4量化存在10.6%的召回崩溃问题，构成部署硬约束，并建立了性能指标到安全目标的映射分析。

Conclusion: 该语义观察层架构在嵌入式AI自动驾驶平台上具备部署前的可行性，能在控制循环外有效监控语义边缘情况并触发安全接管。

Abstract: Semantic anomalies-context-dependent hazards that pixel-level detectors cannot reason about-pose a critical safety risk in autonomous driving. We propose a \emph{semantic observer layer}: a quantized vision-language model (VLM) running at 1--2\,Hz alongside the primary AV control loop, monitoring for semantic edge cases, and triggering fail-safe handoffs when detected. Using Nvidia Cosmos-Reason1-7B with NVFP4 quantization and FlashAttention2, we achieve ~500 ms inference a ~50x speedup over the unoptimized FP16 baseline (no quantization, standard PyTorch attention) on the same hardware--satisfying the observer timing budget. We benchmark accuracy, latency, and quantization behavior in static and video conditions, identify NF4 recall collapse (10.6%) as a hard deployment constraint, and a hazard analysis mapping performance metrics to safety goals. The results establish a pre-deployment feasibility case for the semantic observer architecture on embodied-AI AV platforms.

</details>


### [133] [Bootstrap Perception Under Hardware Depth Failure for Indoor Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28890)
*Nishant Pushparaju,Vivek Mattam,Aliasghar Arab*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种在深度传感器硬件失效情况下仍能实现室内机器人导航的自举感知系统，通过利用有效深度像素对单目深度进行度量尺度校准，实现了传感器失效区域的自我填补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决室内环境中因反射表面导致深度相机大量像素失效，且2D LiDAR无法感知扫描平面上方障碍物的问题。

Method: 构建一个故障感知的感知层级架构：以LiDAR为几何锚点，在有效区域保留硬件深度，在失效区域使用经校准的单目学习深度进行补充；利用幸存的有效深度像素将单目深度估计结果标定到真实尺度。

Result: 在走廊和动态行人场景中，相比仅用LiDAR，选择性融合使代价地图障碍物覆盖率提升55%-110%；轻量化学生模型在Jetson Orin Nano上达到218 FPS，在闭环仿真中导航成功率为9/10，零碰撞，性能接近真值深度基线。

Conclusion: 该系统能在深度传感器部分失效的情况下，通过自我校准和选择性融合维持高性能导航，无需外部数据即可填补感知空白，兼顾安全性与效率。

Abstract: We present a bootstrap perception system for indoor robot navigation under hardware depth failure. In our corridor data, the time-of-flight camera loses up to 78% of its depth pixels on reflective surfaces, yet a 2D LiDAR alone cannot sense obstacles above its scan plane. Our system exploits a self-referential property of this failure: the sensor's surviving valid pixels calibrate learned monocular depth to metric scale, so the system fills its own gaps without external data. The architecture forms a failure-aware sensing hierarchy, conservative when sensors work and filling in when they fail: LiDAR remains the geometric anchor, hardware depth is kept where valid, and learned depth enters only where needed. In corridor and dynamic pedestrian evaluations, selective fusion increases costmap obstacle coverage by 55-110% over LiDAR alone. A compact distilled student runs at 218\,FPS on a Jetson Orin Nano and achieves 9/10 navigation success with zero collisions in closed-loop simulation, matching the ground-truth depth baseline at a fraction of the foundation model's cost.

</details>


### [134] [Robust Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Small UAS Separation Assurance under GPS Degradation and Spoofing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28900)
*Alex Zongo,Filippos Fotiadis,Ufuk Topcu,Peng Wei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多智能体强化学习（MARL）的鲁棒小无人机系统（sUAS）分离保障方法，以应对GPS信号退化和欺骗攻击。通过将状态观测污染建模为智能体与对手之间的零和博弈，并推导出对抗扰动的闭式表达，避免了对抗训练，实现了线性时间评估。该方法在高密度仿真中表现出强鲁棒性，在高达35%的污染率下仍保持接近零碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPS信号在无人机协同监视中的易受干扰和欺骗问题严重影响空中交通状态的可靠性，亟需提升系统在感知状态被破坏时的安全性和鲁棒性。

Method: 将状态观测污染建模为智能体与对手间的零和博弈；推导对抗扰动的闭式表达式，用于线性时间评估；结合KL正则化分析安全性能差距；将闭式对抗策略集成到MARL策略梯度算法中，训练出鲁棒的应对策略。

Result: 闭式对抗扰动具有二阶精度逼近真实最坏情况；安全性能差距在KL正则化下最多随污染概率线性增长；在高密度sUAS仿真中，即使在35%的污染水平下，所提方法仍实现接近零的碰撞率，优于无对抗训练的基线策略。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于闭式对抗建模的MARL方法能有效提升sUAS在GPS退化和欺骗环境下的分离保障能力，具备高效、可分析且强鲁棒的优点，适用于高密度无人机运行场景。

Abstract: We address robust separation assurance for small Unmanned Aircraft Systems (sUAS) under GPS degradation and spoofing via Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL). In cooperative surveillance, each aircraft (or agent) broadcasts its GPS-derived position; when such position broadcasts are corrupted, the entire observed air traffic state becomes unreliable. We cast this state observation corruption as a zero-sum game between the agents and an adversary: with probability R, the adversary perturbs the observed state to maximally degrade each agent's safety performance. We derive a closed-form expression for this adversarial perturbation, bypassing adversarial training entirely and enabling linear-time evaluation in the state dimension. We show that this expression approximates the true worst-case adversarial perturbation with second-order accuracy. We further bound the safety performance gap between clean and corrupted observations, showing that it degrades at most linearly with the corruption probability under Kullback-Leibler regularization. Finally, we integrate the closed-form adversarial policy into a MARL policy gradient algorithm to obtain a robust counter-policy for the agents. In a high-density sUAS simulation, we observe near-zero collision rates under corruption levels up to 35%, outperforming a baseline policy trained without adversarial perturbations.

</details>


### [135] [See Something, Say Something: Context-Criticality-Aware Mobile Robot Communication for Hazard Mitigations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28901)
*Bhavya Oza,Devam Shah,Ghanashyama Prabhu,Devika Kodi,Aliasghar Arab*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于视觉语言模型（VLM/LLM）的感知框架，使自主移动机器人（AMR）能够根据情境敏感地评估危险的严重性、时间紧迫性和缓解可行性，并自适应生成响应消息，从而在安全关键场景中加快响应速度并提升用户信任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键场景中，自主移动机器人需要及时识别并传达潜在危险。传统的固定优先级响应机制缺乏对情境的敏感性，可能导致响应延迟或不恰当，增加风险。因此，需要一种能够动态评估危险程度并生成适当响应的系统。

Method: 提出一个框架，利用VLM/LLM进行上下文感知的危险评估，根据物体所处环境（如厨房中的刀具 vs. 走廊中的刀具）判断其临界性，并生成相应级别的警报信息（如平静提示或紧急协调警报）。通过超过60次巡逻机器人实验验证该方法的有效性。

Result: 与固定优先级基线相比，该方法不仅实现了更快的响应速度，还将用户信任度提升至82%。实验结果表明，结构化的临界性评估能有效提高响应速度和干预效果。

Conclusion: 基于VLM/LLM的情境感知评估框架能够显著提升自主移动机器人在紧急情况下的沟通效率和响应适配性，兼顾时效性与用户信任，是实现安全可靠人机协作的关键路径。

Abstract: The proverb ``see something, say something'' captures a core responsibility of autonomous mobile robots in safety-critical situations: when they detect a hazard, they must communicate--and do so quickly. In emergency scenarios, delayed or miscalibrated responses directly increase the time to action and the risk of damage. We argue that a systematic context-sensitive assessment of the criticality level, time sensitivity, and feasibility of mitigation is necessary for AMRs to reduce time to action and respond effectively. This paper presents a framework in which VLM/LLM-based perception drives adaptive message generation, for example, a knife in a kitchen produces a calm acknowledgment; the same object in a corridor triggers an urgent coordinated alert. Validation in 60+ runs using a patrolling mobile robot not only empowers faster response, but also brings user trusts to 82\% compared to fixed-priority baselines, validating that structured criticality assessment improves both response speed and mitigation effectiveness.

</details>


### [136] [Why That Robot? A Qualitative Analysis of Justification Strategies for Robot Color Selection Across Occupational Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28919)
*Jiangen He,Wanqi Zhang,Jessica K. Barfield*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了在不同职业背景下，用户如何基于机器人的肤色和拟人化特征进行选择，并揭示了隐性社会偏见如何通过功能主义等表面理性化的理由影响决策。研究表明，尽管功能性是主要考量因素，但种族和职业刻板印象仍系统性地渗透其中，且这种偏见常在无意识中被合理化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人越来越多地进入劳动力市场，理解人类对机器人外貌（如肤色和拟人化程度）的偏好及其背后的隐性社会偏见变得至关重要，以避免技术加剧现有社会不平等。

Method: 通过对1,038名参与者提供的4,146条开放式理由进行定性分析，构建并验证了一个多维度编码方案（κ=0.73），研究用户在四种职业情境下对不同肤色和形态机器人的选择逻辑。

Result: 功能性主义是主导解释策略（52%），但实际选择受种族与职业刻板印象系统性影响；刻板印象启动实验显示，肤色选择会受潜意识影响，但用户仍用情感或任务相关理由掩饰；用户人口学背景显著影响其推理方式；当机器人越拟人化时，用户越倾向于采用‘机器中心’去种族化解释。

Conclusion: 设计劳动力机器人时需警惕隐性偏见的复制，研究提供了减少社会偏见传播的具体设计建议，强调应关注用户理性化话语背后的深层认知机制。

Abstract: As robots increasingly enter the workforce, human-robot interaction (HRI) must address how implicit social biases influence user preferences. This paper investigates how users rationalize their selections of robots varying in skin tone and anthropomorphic features across different occupations. By qualitatively analyzing 4,146 open-ended justifications from 1,038 participants, we map the reasoning frameworks driving robot color selection across four professional contexts. We developed and validated a comprehensive, multidimensional coding scheme via human--AI consensus ($κ= 0.73$). Our results demonstrate that while utilitarian \textit{Functionalism} is the dominant justification strategy (52\%), participants systematically adapted these practical rationales to align with established racial and occupational stereotypes. Furthermore, we reveal that bias frequently operates beneath conscious rationalization: exposure to racial stereotype primes significantly shifted participants' color choices, yet their spoken justifications remained masked by standard affective or task-related reasoning. We also found that demographic backgrounds significantly shape justification strategies, and that robot shape strongly modulates color interpretation. Specifically, as robots become highly anthropomorphic, users increasingly retreat from functional reasoning toward \textit{Machine-Centric} de-racialization. Through these empirical results, we provide actionable design implications to help reduce the perpetuation of societal biases in future workforce robots.

</details>


### [137] [World2Rules: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Learning World-Governing Safety Rules for Aviation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28952)
*Haichuan Wang,Jay Patrikar,Sebastian Scherer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: World2Rules 是一个神经符号框架，用于从真实多模态航空数据中学习世界治理的安全规则，结合神经模型与符号方法，通过分层反思推理生成紧凑、可解释的一阶逻辑规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中安全关键系统的规则复杂且依赖上下文，手动编写易出错；直接从含噪声、稀疏故障的多模态数据中学习规则具有挑战性。

Method: 提出 World2Rules 框架，利用神经模型提取候选符号事实，使用归纳逻辑编程作为验证层，并通过分层反思推理确保跨示例、子集和规则的一致性，过滤不可靠证据并修剪 unsupported 假设。

Result: 在真实航空安全数据上评估显示，该方法比纯神经模型 F1 提高 23.6%，比单次神经符号基线提高 43.2%。

Conclusion: World2Rules 能有效从嘈杂多模态数据中学习出紧凑、可解释且适用于安全关键系统的形式化安全规则。

Abstract: Many real-world safety-critical systems are governed by explicit rules that define unsafe world configurations and constrain agent interactions. In practice, these rules are complex and context-dependent, making manual specification incomplete and error-prone. Learning such rules from real-world multimodal data is further challenged by noise, inconsistency, and sparse failure cases. Neural models can extract structure from text and visual data but lack formal guarantees, while symbolic methods provide verifiability yet are brittle when applied directly to imperfect observations. We present World2Rules, a neuro-symbolic framework for learning world-governing safety rules from real-world multimodal aviation data. World2Rules learns from both nominal operational data and aviation crash and incident reports, treating neural models as proposal mechanisms for candidate symbolic facts and inductive logic programming as a verification layer. The framework employs hierarchical reflective reasoning, enforcing consistency across examples, subsets, and rules to filter unreliable evidence, aggregate only mutually consistent components, and prune unsupported hypotheses. This design limits error propagation from noisy neural extractions and yields compact, interpretable first-order logic rules that characterize unsafe world configurations. We evaluate World2Rules on real-world aviation safety data and show that it learns rules that achieve 23.6% higher F1 score than purely neural and 43.2% higher F1 score than single-pass neuro-symbolic baseline, while remaining suitable for safety-critical reasoning and formal analysis.

</details>


### [138] [AutoWorld: Scaling Multi-Agent Traffic Simulation with Self-Supervised World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28963)
*Mozhgan Pourkeshavatz,Tianran Liu,Nicholas Rhinehart*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为AutoWorld的交通仿真框架，利用未标记的LiDAR数据中的占据表示来学习世界模型，通过粗到细的预测场景上下文和级联行列式点过程提升多智能体运动生成的真实性和多样性，在WOSAC基准上取得了最佳表现，并验证了无标签数据对仿真性能的持续提升作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于监督学习的数据驱动交通仿真方法依赖大量标注轨迹或语义信息，成本高且难以扩展；而海量未标记传感器数据未被充分利用。本文旨在探索如何有效利用这些未标记数据来提升交通仿真的真实性和可扩展性。

Method: 提出AutoWorld框架：1）从无标签LiDAR数据中学习占据表示的世界模型；2）构建由该世界模型生成样本引导的粗到细预测场景上下文，作为多智能体运动生成模型的输入；3）采用级联行列式点过程（DPP）框架指导世界模型与运动模型的采样，以增强样本多样性；4）设计运动感知的潜在监督目标，强化对场景动态的表征能力。

Result: 在WOSAC基准测试中，AutoWorld在主要指标Realism Meta Metric（RMM）上排名第一；实验表明加入无标签LiDAR数据能持续提升仿真性能；消融研究验证了各组件的有效性。

Conclusion: AutoWorld成功利用无标签LiDAR数据提升了交通仿真系统的逼真度和可扩展性，为无需额外标注的大规模仿真提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Multi-agent traffic simulation is central to developing and testing autonomous driving systems. Recent data-driven simulators have achieved promising results, but rely heavily on supervised learning from labeled trajectories or semantic annotations, making it costly to scale their performance. Meanwhile, large amounts of unlabeled sensor data can be collected at scale but remain largely unused by existing traffic simulation frameworks. This raises a key question: How can a method harness unlabeled data to improve traffic simulation performance? In this work, we propose AutoWorld, a traffic simulation framework that employs a world model learned from unlabeled occupancy representations of LiDAR data. Given world model samples, AutoWorld constructs a coarse-to-fine predictive scene context as input to a multi-agent motion generation model. To promote sample diversity, AutoWorld uses a cascaded Determinantal Point Process framework to guide the sampling processes of both the world model and the motion model. Furthermore, we designed a motion-aware latent supervision objective that enhances AutoWorld's representation of scene dynamics. Experiments on the WOSAC benchmark show that AutoWorld ranks first on the leaderboard according to the primary Realism Meta Metric (RMM). We further show that simulation performance consistently improves with the inclusion of unlabeled LiDAR data, and study the efficacy of each component with ablations. Our method paves the way for scaling traffic simulation realism without additional labeling. Our project page contains additional visualizations and released code.

</details>


### [139] [Large Neighborhood Search for Multi-Agent Task Assignment and Path Finding with Precedence Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28968)
*Viraj Parimi,Brian C. Williams*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了任务分配与带优先约束的路径规划问题（TAPF-PC），并设计了一种基于大邻域搜索的方法来联合优化任务分配、优先级满足和路径成本，在多个基准测试中显著优于固定分配方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多多机器人应用不仅要求任务按正确顺序执行，还要求合理分配任务以提升整体效率，而现有方法未同时优化任务分配与路径规划。

Method: 提出TAPF-PC框架，并采用基于重分配的大邻域搜索方法，从可行的MAPF-PC初始解出发，通过迭代修复邻域解来联合优化任务分配与路径规划。

Result: 实验表明，最佳配置在89.1%的实例中优于固定分配的初始解，验证了该方法在优先约束下灵活重分配的有效性。

Conclusion: 大邻域搜索能有效捕捉在优先约束下任务重分配带来的性能增益，为多机器人任务协同提供了更优解决方案。

Abstract: Many multi-robot applications require tasks to be completed efficiently and in the correct order, so that downstream operations can proceed at the right time. Multi-agent path finding with precedence constraints (MAPF-PC) is a well-studied framework for computing collision-free plans that satisfy ordering relations when task sequences are fixed in advance. In many applications, however, solution quality depends not only on how agents move, but also on which agent performs which task. This motivates the lifted problem of task assignment and path finding with precedence constraints (TAPF-PC), which extends MAPF-PC by jointly optimizing assignment, precedence satisfaction, and routing cost. To address the resulting coupled TAPF-PC search space, we develop a large neighborhood search approach that starts from a feasible MAPF-PC seed and iteratively improves it through reassignment-based neighborhood repair, restoring feasibility within each selected neighborhood. Experiments across multiple benchmark families and scaling regimes show that the best-performing configuration improves 89.1% of instances over fixed-assignment seed solutions, demonstrating that large neighborhood search effectively captures the gains from flexible reassignment under precedence constraints.

</details>


### [140] [Gleanmer: A 6 mW SoC for Real-Time 3D Gaussian Occupancy Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29005)
*Zih-Sing Fu,Peter Zhi Xuan Li,Sertac Karaman,Vivienne Sze*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Gleanmer是首个在6mW以下实现边缘设备实时3D占用映射的SoC，通过高斯算法-硬件协同优化显著降低能耗和面积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真3D占用映射对AR/VR和自主导航等边缘应用至关重要，但受限于功耗。

Method: 提出Gleanmer，一种基于高斯表示的3D占用图（GMMap）的SoC，采用算法-硬件协同优化以实现高效的直接计算和数据复用，并支持高斯的近似计算。

Result: 在16nm CMOS上实现，建图能效降低63%，查询能效降低81%，加速器面积减少38%；支持超过88fps的640x480图像实时处理，查询速度超54万坐标/秒，整系统功耗低于6mW。

Conclusion: Gleanmer是首个在低于6mW功耗下实现边缘端实时3D占用映射的流片SoC，为低功耗高精度三维感知提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: High-fidelity 3D occupancy mapping is essential for many edge-based applications (such as AR/VR and autonomous navigation) but is limited by power constraints. We present Gleanmer, a system on chip (SoC) with an accelerator for GMMap, a 3D occupancy map using Gaussians. Through algorithm-hardware co-optimizations for direct computation and efficient reuse of these compact Gaussians, Gleanmer reduces construction and query energy by up to 63% and 81%, respectively. Approximate computation on Gaussians reduces accelerator area by 38%. Using 16nm CMOS, Gleanmer processes 640x480 images in real time beyond 88 fps during map construction and processes over 540K coordinates per second during map query. To our knowledge, Gleanmer is the first fabricated SoC to achieve real-time 3D occupancy mapping under 6 mW for edge-based applications.

</details>


### [141] [Efficient Camera Pose Augmentation for View Generalization in Robotic Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29192)
*Sen Wang,Huaiyi Dong,Jingyi Tian,Jiayi Li,Zhuo Yang,Tongtong Cao,Anlin Chen,Shuang Wu,Le Wang,Sanping Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GenSplat是一种基于3D高斯点阵的前馈框架，通过渲染新视角实现视图泛化的策略学习，提升2D视觉运动策略在未见视角下的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的2D中心化视觉运动策略因依赖静态观测，在面对新视角时动作映射不一致，缺乏良好的视角泛化能力。

Method: 提出GenSplat框架，采用置换等变架构从稀疏未标定输入中单次前向重建高保真3D场景，并设计3D先验蒸馏策略来正则化3DGS优化过程，避免几何坍塌；利用稳定3D表示渲染多样合成视角，在训练中系统增强观测流形。

Result: 所提方法使策略基于底层3D结构做决策，在严重空间扰动下仍能稳健执行，显著优于基线模型。

Conclusion: GenSplat通过引入稳定的3D场景表示与多视角渲染增强，有效提升了 visuomotor 策略的视角泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Prevailing 2D-centric visuomotor policies exhibit a pronounced deficiency in novel view generalization, as their reliance on static observations hinders consistent action mapping across unseen views. In response, we introduce GenSplat, a feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting framework that facilitates view-generalized policy learning through novel view rendering. GenSplat employs a permutation-equivariant architecture to reconstruct high-fidelity 3D scenes from sparse, uncalibrated inputs in a single forward pass. To ensure structural integrity, we design a 3D-prior distillation strategy that regularizes the 3DGS optimization, preventing the geometric collapse typical of purely photometric supervision. By rendering diverse synthetic views from these stable 3D representations, we systematically augment the observational manifold during training. This augmentation forces the policy to ground its decisions in underlying 3D structures, thereby ensuring robust execution under severe spatial perturbations where baselines severely degrade.

</details>


### [142] [Kilohertz-Safe: A Scalable Framework for Constrained Dexterous Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29213)
*Yinxiao Tian,Ziyi Yang,Zinan Zhao,Zhen Kan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种可扩展的运动重定向框架，通过将非线性问题转化为关节微分空间中的凸二次规划，实现高频实时操作与安全约束的兼顾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在计算成本或安全性保证方面存在不足，难以同时满足高频率实时控制和异构运动学及安全约束的需求。

Method: 将运动重定向问题重新表述为关节微分空间中的凸二次规划，通过系统线性化引入异构约束，并集成控制屏障函数以提供形式化的安全保证。

Result: 在Wuji Hand平台上验证了该框架的有效性，平均延迟为9.05毫秒，超过95%的重定向帧满足安全标准，在复杂操作任务中有效避免自碰撞和穿透。

Conclusion: 所提框架在计算效率、数值稳定性和安全性之间取得了良好平衡，显著优于Dex-Retargeting和GeoRT等现有方法。

Abstract: Dexterous hand teleoperation requires motion re-targeting methods that simultaneously achieve high-frequency real-time performance and enforcement of heterogeneous kinematic and safety constraints. Existing nonlinear optimization-based approaches often incur prohibitive computational cost, limiting their applicability to kilohertz-level control, while learning-based methods typically lack formal safety guarantees. This paper proposes a scalable motion retargeting framework that reformulates the nonlinear retargeting problem into a convex quadratic program in joint differential space. Heterogeneous constraints, including kinematic limits and collision avoidance, are incorporated through systematic linearization, resulting in improved computational efficiency and numerical stability. Control barrier functions are further integrated to provide formal safety guarantees during the retargeting process. The proposed framework is validated through simulations and hardware experiments on the Wuji Hand platform, outperforming state-of-the-art methods such as Dex-Retargeting and GeoRT. The framework achieves high-frequency operation with an average latency of 9.05 ms, while over 95% of retargeted frames satisfy the safety criteria, effectively mitigating self-collision and penetration during complex manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [143] [Long-Reach Robotic Manipulation for Assembly and Outfitting of Lunar Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29226)
*Stanley Wang,Venny Kojouharov,Long Yin Chung,Daniel Morton,Mark Cutkosky*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于月球表面基础设施建设的紧凑型长距离机械臂，采用可展开复合材料臂结构，具备在大范围工作空间内进行电缆布线等精细操作的能力，并通过控制策略抑制结构变形和振动，实验显示末端定位误差小于15 mm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为支持月球表面半自主或全自主机器人基础设施建设，特别是大尺度结构上的电缆敷设等任务，需要具备长距离作业能力且能进行精细操作的机械臂系统。

Method: 设计并开发了一种集成可展开复合材料臂的紧凑型长距离机械臂，分析其展开过程中的挠曲、振动和花瓣状变形特性，并提出相应的操作控制策略以抑制这些不利影响。

Result: 实验结果表明，在最大1.8米的臂长下，机械臂末端平均定位误差小于15毫米，并成功完成了电缆布线任务验证。

Conclusion: 该长距离可展开机械臂具有在月球等极端环境下执行大范围精细操作任务的潜力，适用于未来月面设施的电气化建设需求。

Abstract: Future infrastructure construction on the lunar surface will require semi- or fully-autonomous operation from robots deployed at the build site. In particular, tasks such as electrical outfitting necessitate transport, routing, and fine manipulation of cables across large structures. To address this need, we present a compact and long-reach manipulator incorporating a deployable composite boom, capable of performing manipulation tasks across large structures and workspaces. We characterize the deflection, vibration, and blossoming characteristics inherent to the deployable structure, and present a manipulation control strategy to mitigate these effects. Experiments indicate an average endpoint accuracy error of less than 15 mm for boom lengths up to 1.8 m. We demonstrate the approach with a cable routing task to illustrate the potential for lunar outfitting applications that benefit from long reach.

</details>


### [144] [Long-Reach Robotic Cleaning for Lunar Solar Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29240)
*Stanley Wang,Velin Kojouharov,Long Yin Chung,Daniel Morton,Mark Cutkosky*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种配备长距离、轻量化可展开臂和可更换清洁工具的小型移动机器人，用于月球表面太阳能电池板的自主清洁，通过柔顺腕部和力控算法实现稳定接触清洁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球尘埃积累会快速降低太阳能电池板输出，影响任务寿命，亟需可靠的表面基础设施维护方案。

Method: 开发一种具有长距离可展开臂的小型移动机器人，集成柔顺腕部与远端力传感，采用基于速度的导纳控制器调节清洁过程中的接触力。

Result: 在0.3至1.0米臂长范围内，系统能维持约2N的法向力进行清洁，初始接触后力控制RMS误差约为0.2N。

Conclusion: 可展开长距离机械臂是执行月球基础设施（如太阳能板、散热器和光学表面）机器人化维护的有前景架构。

Abstract: Commercial lunar activity is accelerating the need for reliable surface infrastructure and routine operations to keep it functioning. Maintenance tasks such as inspection, cleaning, dust mitigation, and minor repair are essential to preserve performance and extend system life. A specific application is the cleaning of lunar solar arrays. Solar arrays are expected to provide substantial fraction of lunar surface power and operate for months to years, supplying continuous energy to landers, habitats, and surface assets, making sustained output mission-critical. However, over time lunar dust accumulates on these large solar arrays, which can rapidly degrade panel output and reduce mission lifetime. We propose a small mobile robot equipped with a long-reach, lightweight deployable boom and interchangeable cleaning tool to perform gentle cleaning over meter-scale workspaces with minimal human involvement. Building on prior vision-guided long-reach manipulation, we add a compliant wrist with distal force sensing and a velocity-based admittance controller to regulate stable contact during surface cleaning. In preliminary benchtop experiments on a planar surface, the system maintained approximately 2 N normal force while executing a simple cleaning motion over boom lengths from 0.3 m to 1.0 m, with RMS force error of approximately 0.2 N after initial contact. These early results suggest that deployable long-reach manipulators are a promising architecture for robotic maintenance of lunar infrastructure such as solar arrays, radiators, and optical surfaces.

</details>


### [145] [IMPASTO: Integrating Model-Based Planning with Learned Dynamics Models for Robotic Oil Painting Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29315)
*Yingke Wang,Hao Li,Yifeng Zhu,Hong-Xing Yu,Ken Goldberg,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu,Yunzhu Li,Ruohan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IMPASTO是一个无需人类示范的机器人油画系统，通过自学习像素动态模型与基于模型的规划，实现油彩画作的高精度复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在缺乏人类逐步示范和精确仿真器的情况下，使机器人能够自主推断并执行油画复现所需的笔触轨迹、力度和颜色。

Method: 结合学习到的像素动态模型与模型预测控制（MPC）进行滚动时域规划，使用力敏控制器在7自由度机械臂上执行笔触，并通过机器人自玩数据进行训练。

Result: 系统能准确模拟单笔画和多笔艺术作品，在复现精度上优于基线方法。

Conclusion: IMPASTO实现了无需人类示范的高质量机器人油画复现，整合了低力度控制、学习动态模型与高层闭环规划。

Abstract: Robotic reproduction of oil paintings using soft brushes and pigments requires force-sensitive control of deformable tools, prediction of brushstroke effects, and multi-step stroke planning, often without human step-by-step demonstrations or faithful simulators. Given only a sequence of target oil painting images, can a robot infer and execute the stroke trajectories, forces, and colors needed to reproduce it? We present IMPASTO, a robotic oil-painting system that integrates learned pixel dynamics models with model-based planning. The dynamics models predict canvas updates from image observations and parameterized stroke actions; a receding-horizon model predictive control optimizer then plans trajectories and forces, while a force-sensitive controller executes strokes on a 7-DoF robot arm. IMPASTO integrates low-level force control, learned dynamics models, and high-level closed-loop planning, learns solely from robot self-play, and approximates human artists' single-stroke datasets and multi-stroke artworks, outperforming baselines in reproduction accuracy. Project website: https://impasto-robopainting.github.io/

</details>


### [146] [Industrial-Grade Robust Robot Vision for Screw Detection and Removal under Uneven Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29363)
*Tomoki Ishikura,Genichiro Matsuda,Takuya Kiyokawa,Kensuke Harada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种自动化系统，用于拆解空调室外机，结合两阶段检测方法和基于格点的局部校准策略，在实际验证中实现了78.3%的拆解成功率和平均193秒的周期时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于日本劳动力减少而家用电器使用量增加，回收工厂需要自动化拆解流程；空调室外机因尺寸差异大、污垢和锈蚀严重，自动化拆解面临挑战。

Method: 提出一种集成任务特定的两阶段检测方法和基于格点的局部校准策略的自动化系统，实现高精度螺丝检测与机械操作。

Result: 螺丝检测召回率达到99.8%，操作精度达到±0.75毫米，无需预编程坐标；在120台设备上的实测中，拆解成功率为78.3%，平均周期时间为193秒。

Conclusion: 该系统在真实工业环境中表现出良好的可行性，具备应用于家电回收产业的潜力。

Abstract: As the amount of used home appliances is expected to increase despite the decreasing labor force in Japan, there is a need to automate disassembling processes at recycling plants. The automation of disassembling air conditioner outdoor units, however, remains a challenge due to unit size variations and exposure to dirt and rust. To address these challenges, this study proposes an automated system that integrates a task-specific two-stage detection method and a lattice-based local calibration strategy. This approach achieved a screw detection recall of 99.8% despite severe degradation and ensured a manipulation accuracy of +/-0.75 mm without pre-programmed coordinates. In real-world validation with 120 units, the system attained a disassembly success rate of 78.3% and an average cycle time of 193 seconds, confirming its feasibility for industrial application.

</details>


### [147] [Interacting Multiple Model Proprioceptive Odometry for Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29383)
*Wanlei Li,Zichang Chen,Shilei Li,Xiaogang Xiong,Yunjiang Lou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于交互多模型（IMM）的本体感知里程计框架，通过融合多种接触假设来提升足式机器人在不同接触条件下的状态估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有本体感知里程计方法多依赖理想点接触假设，而在实际运动中该假设常被违反，导致估计性能下降。因此需要一种能适应复杂接触情况的鲁棒状态估计方法。

Method: 采用交互多模型（IMM）框架，结合多个接触模式假设，在统一的概率框架下实现在线模式切换与概率融合，利用本体感知信息进行状态估计。

Result: 在仿真和真实实验中，所提方法相比现有最先进方法显著提升了位姿估计精度，同时保持了相当的计算效率。

Conclusion: 所提出的IMM-based proprioceptive odometry框架能够有效应对非理想接触条件，增强了足式机器人状态估计的鲁棒性和准确性，适用于传感器退化或外部感知不可靠的场景。

Abstract: State estimation for legged robots remains challenging because legged odometry generally suffers from limited observability and therefore depends critically on measurement constraints to suppress drift. When exteroceptive sensors are unreliable or degraded, such constraints are mainly derived from proprioceptive measurements, particularly contact-related leg kinematics information. However, most existing proprioceptive odometry methods rely on an idealized point-contact assumption, which is often violated during real locomotion. Consequently, the effectiveness of proprioceptive constraints may be significantly reduced, resulting in degraded estimation accuracy. To address these limitations, we propose an interacting multiple model (IMM)-based proprioceptive odometry framework for legged robots. By incorporating multiple contact hypotheses within a unified probabilistic framework, the proposed method enables online mode switching and probabilistic fusion under varying contact conditions. Extensive simulations and real-world experiments demonstrate that the proposed method achieves superior pose estimation accuracy over state-of-the-art methods while maintaining comparable computational efficiency.

</details>


### [148] [Learning Semantic Priorities for Autonomous Target Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29391)
*Max Lodel,Nils Wilde,Robert Babuška,Javier Alonso-Mora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种利用专家知识训练语义优先级模型的目标搜索方法，通过结合前沿探索规划和组合优化，在未知环境中实现更高效的目标搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标搜索方法依赖于相似领域的大量数据训练，难以适应多样化环境；而人类专家具备高层语义知识，可有效引导机器人在未知环境中搜索目标。

Method: 利用模拟的专家指导生成合成数据集，训练语义优先级模型，并将其应用于基于组合优化的前沿探索规划器中，以语义特征驱动搜索过程。

Result: 在未见过的环境中进行仿真测试，结果表明该方法相比仅以覆盖率为驱动的探索方法能更快地找到目标。

Conclusion: 所提出的语义优先级模型能够有效提升机器人在未知多样环境中目标搜索的效率，同时保持探索的鲁棒性和完整性。

Abstract: The use of semantic features can improve the efficiency of target search in unknown environments for robotic search and rescue missions. Current target search methods rely on training with large datasets of similar domains, which limits the adaptability to diverse environments. However, human experts possess high-level knowledge about semantic relationships necessary to effectively guide a robot during target search missions in diverse and previously unseen environments. In this paper, we propose a target search method that leverages expert input to train a model of semantic priorities. By employing the learned priorities in a frontier exploration planner using combinatorial optimization, our approach achieves efficient target search driven by semantic features while ensuring robustness and complete coverage. The proposed semantic priority model is trained with several synthetic datasets of simulated expert guidance for target search. Simulation tests in previously unseen environments show that our method consistently achieves faster target recovery than a coverage-driven exploration planner.

</details>


### [149] [CLaD: Planning with Grounded Foresight via Cross-Modal Latent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29409)
*Andrew Jeong,Jaemin Kim,Sebin Lee,Sung-Eui Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CLaD框架，通过不对称跨模态注意力建模机器人操作中本体感知与语义状态的联合演化，结合自监督目标与扩散策略，在LIBERO-LONG上达到94.7%成功率，性能接近大型视觉语言动作模型但参数更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在规划时未显式对齐运动学与语义模态间的转换，导致跨模态信息融合不充分。

Method: 提出CLaD框架，采用不对称交叉注意力机制，使运动学状态可查询语义状态；通过EMA目标编码器和辅助重建损失进行自监督训练，预测具身化的潜在预见，并将预测结果与观测融合以调制扩散策略生成动作。

Result: 在LIBERO-LONG基准上实现了94.7%的任务成功率，表现优于或媲美现有的大规模视觉语言动作模型，同时模型参数显著更少。

Conclusion: CLaD有效联合建模了运动学与语义状态的动态演化，提升了跨模态表示的学习能力，在复杂长视野任务中实现了高效且鲁棒的机器人操作。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation involves kinematic and semantic transitions that are inherently coupled via underlying actions. However, existing approaches plan within either semantic or latent space without explicitly aligning these cross-modal transitions. To address this, we propose CLaD, a framework that models how proprioceptive and semantic states jointly evolve under actions through asymmetric cross-attention that allows kinematic transitions to query semantic ones. CLaD predicts grounded latent foresights via self-supervised objectives with EMA target encoders and auxiliary reconstruction losses, preventing representation collapse while anchoring predictions to observable states. Predicted foresights are modulated with observations to condition a diffusion policy for action generation. On LIBERO-LONG benchmark, CLaD achieves 94.7\% success rate, competitive with large VLAs with significantly fewer parameters.

</details>


### [150] [RAAP: Retrieval-Augmented Affordance Prediction with Cross-Image Action Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29419)
*Qiyuan Zhuang,He-Yang Xu,Yijun Wang,Xin-Yang Zhao,Yang-Yang Li,Xiu-Shen Wei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为RAAP的检索增强型可操作性预测框架，通过结合检索与对齐学习，实现了在少量样本下对未见物体和类别的稳定泛化，并支持零样本机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理未见类别时存在接触点定位不准或动作预测错误的问题，且依赖大规模模型或易受数据稀疏影响的检索方法，难以实现鲁棒的泛化。

Method: RAAP将静态接触点定位与动态动作方向预测解耦：通过密集对应传递接触点，利用基于检索增强的对齐模型结合多个参考样本，并采用双加权注意力机制预测动作方向。模型仅需每任务数十个样本，在DROID和HOI4D的子集上训练。

Result: RAAP在模拟和真实世界中均实现了跨未见物体和类别的稳定性能，并展现出零样本机器人操作能力。

Conclusion: RAAP通过融合检索与对齐学习，在小样本条件下实现了对物体可操作性的鲁棒预测，推动了机器人在非结构化环境中的泛化交互能力。

Abstract: Understanding object affordances is essential for enabling robots to perform purposeful and fine-grained interactions in diverse and unstructured environments. However, existing approaches either rely on retrieval, which is fragile due to sparsity and coverage gaps, or on large-scale models, which frequently mislocalize contact points and mispredict post-contact actions when applied to unseen categories, thereby hindering robust generalization. We introduce Retrieval-Augmented Affordance Prediction (RAAP), a framework that unifies affordance retrieval with alignment-based learning. By decoupling static contact localization and dynamic action direction, RAAP transfers contact points via dense correspondence and predicts action directions through a retrieval-augmented alignment model that consolidates multiple references with dual-weighted attention. Trained on compact subsets of DROID and HOI4D with as few as tens of samples per task, RAAP achieves consistent performance across unseen objects and categories, and enables zero-shot robotic manipulation in both simulation and the real world. Project website: https://github.com/SEU-VIPGroup/RAAP.

</details>


### [151] [CReF: Cross-modal and Recurrent Fusion for Depth-conditioned Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29452)
*Yuan Hao,Ruiqi Yu,Shixin Luo,Guoteng Zhang,Jun Wu,Qiuguo Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CReF框架，一种无需显式几何中间表示的单阶段深度条件人形机器人运动方法，通过跨模态与循环融合实现复杂地形下的稳定行走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于外部感知的人形机器人运动方法常依赖于显式的几何抽象（如2.5D地形表示或几何相关的监督目标），在面对垂直结构、穿孔障碍和真实复杂环境时存在表征局限性。

Method: 提出CReF框架，直接从前向深度图像中学习运动相关特征；采用本体感觉查询的跨模态注意力机制融合深度与本体感觉信息，通过门控残差融合模块整合特征，并使用带有高速输出门调控的GRU进行时序整合；引入地形感知的落足点奖励机制，从足端点云中提取可支撑候选点以优化落足位置。

Result: 在仿真和真实人形机器人上的实验表明，该方法能在多种复杂地形中实现稳健通行，并能零样本迁移到包含扶手、空心托盘、强反射干扰和视觉杂乱的户外场景的真实环境中。

Conclusion: CReF通过去除显式几何中间表示，实现了更灵活、鲁棒的跨模态感知运动控制，在复杂真实世界地形中表现出优异的通行能力与泛化性能。

Abstract: Stable traversal over geometrically complex terrain increasingly requires exteroceptive perception, yet prior perceptive humanoid locomotion methods often remain tied to explicit geometric abstractions, either by mediating control through robot-centric 2.5D terrain representations or by shaping depth learning with auxiliary geometry-related targets. Such designs inherit the representational bias of the intermediate or supervisory target and can be restrictive for vertical structures, perforated obstacles, and complex real-world clutter. We propose CReF (Cross-modal and Recurrent Fusion), a single-stage depth-conditioned humanoid locomotion framework that learns locomotion-relevant features directly from raw forward-facing depth without explicit geometric intermediates. CReF couples proprioception and depth tokens through proprioception-queried cross-modal attention, fuses the resulting representation with a gated residual fusion block, and performs temporal integration with a Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU) regulated by a highway-style output gate for state-dependent blending of recurrent and feedforward features. To further improve terrain interaction, we introduce a terrain-aware foothold placement reward that extracts supportable foothold candidates from foot-end point-cloud samples and rewards touchdown locations that lie close to the nearest supportable candidate. Experiments in simulation and on a physical humanoid demonstrate robust traversal over diverse terrains and effective zero-shot transfer to real-world scenes containing handrails, hollow pallet assemblies, severe reflective interference, and visually cluttered outdoor surroundings.

</details>


### [152] [Communication Outage-Resistant UUV State Estimation: A Variational History Distillation Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29512)
*Shuyue Li,Miguel López-Benítez,Eng Gee Lim,Fei Ma,Qian Dong,Mengze Cao,Limin Yu,Xiaohui Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种变分历史蒸馏（VHD）方法，用于提升无人水下航行器（UUV）集群在通信中断期间的状态估计鲁棒性，通过融合物理运动模型与历史轨迹提取的虚拟测量，并引入自适应置信机制，显著降低了预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于水声通信易中断，且存在未建模动态因素（如未知洋流），传统状态估计器在通信中断时性能急剧下降，可能导致任务失败。因此需要一种在无通信条件下仍能保持高精度状态估计的方法。

Method: 将轨迹预测建模为近似贝叶斯推理过程，结合基于物理的运动模型与从历史轨迹中蒸馏出的‘虚拟测量’；引入自适应置信机制，随通信中断时间延长动态降低对虚拟测量的信任度。

Result: 在高保真环境中进行的蒙特卡洛仿真表明，在40秒通信中断期间，该方法将预测均方根误差（RMSE）从约170米降至15米，减少了91%。

Conclusion: VHD方法能有效维持UUV集群在完全通信丢失情况下的状态估计性能，显著优于传统方法，具备强鲁棒性和应用潜力。

Abstract: The reliable operation of Unmanned Underwater Vehicle (UUV) clusters is highly dependent on continuous acoustic communication. However, this communication method is highly susceptible to intermittent interruptions. When communication outages occur, standard state estimators such as the Unscented Kalman Filter (UKF) will be forced to make open-loop predictions. If the environment contains unmodeled dynamic factors, such as unknown ocean currents, this estimation error will grow rapidly, which may eventually lead to mission failure. To address this critical issue, this paper proposes a Variational History Distillation (VHD) approach. VHD regards trajectory prediction as an approximate Bayesian reasoning process, which links a standard motion model based on physics with a pattern extracted directly from the past trajectory of the UUV. This is achieved by synthesizing ``virtual measurements'' distilled from historical trajectories. Recognizing that the reliability of extrapolated historical trends degrades over extended prediction horizons, an adaptive confidence mechanism is introduced. This mechanism allows the filter to gradually reduce the trust of virtual measurements as the communication outage time is extended. Extensive Monte Carlo simulations in a high-fidelity environment demonstrate that the proposed method achieves a 91\% reduction in prediction Root Mean Square Error (RMSE), reducing the error from approximately 170 m to 15 m during a 40-second communication outage. These results demonstrate that VHD can maintain robust state estimation performance even under complete communication loss.

</details>


### [153] [GraSP-STL: A Graph-Based Framework for Zero-Shot Signal Temporal Logic Planning via Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29533)
*Ancheng Hou,Ruijia Liu,Xiang Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为GraSP-STL的图搜索框架，用于在无环境交互和任务特定训练的情况下，基于离线数据实现信号时序逻辑（STL）规范的零样本规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在缺乏动态模型和环境交互的离线设定下，如何对未见过的STL任务实现零样本、长视野的规划是一个挑战。

Method: 通过学习目标条件下的值函数构建状态空间的有限视野可达性度量，并构造以代表性状态为节点、短视野转移为边的有向图抽象；规划过程转化为对路径点序列的图搜索，并利用算术-几何平均鲁棒性和区间语义评估，最终由学习到的策略执行。

Result: 实验结果表明该方法在多种离线STL规划任务中有效，支持对未见STL任务的零样本泛化和长视野规划。

Conclusion: GraSP-STL成功地将可重用的可达性学习与任务相关的规划分离，实现了从离线轨迹中进行零样本STL规划的新范式。

Abstract: This paper studies offline, zero-shot planning under Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifications. We assume access only to an offline dataset of state-action-state transitions collected by a task-agnostic behavior policy, with no analytical dynamics model, no further environment interaction, and no task-specific retraining. The objective is to synthesize a control strategy whose resulting trajectory satisfies an arbitrary unseen STL specification. To this end, we propose GraSP-STL, a graph-search-based framework for zero-shot STL planning from offline trajectories. The method learns a goal-conditioned value function from offline data and uses it to induce a finite-horizon reachability metric over the state space. Based on this metric, it constructs a directed graph abstraction whose nodes represent representative states and whose edges encode feasible short-horizon transitions. Planning is then formulated as a graph search over waypoint sequences, evaluated using arithmetic-geometric mean robustness and its interval semantics, and executed by a learned goal-conditioned policy. The proposed framework separates reusable reachability learning from task-conditioned planning, enabling zero-shot generalization to unseen STL tasks and long-horizon planning through the composition of short-horizon behaviors from offline data. Experimental results demonstrate its effectiveness on a range of offline STL planning tasks.

</details>


### [154] [Semantic Zone-Based Map Management for Stable AI-Integrated Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29627)
*Huichang Yun,Seungho Yoo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于语义区域的稠密地图管理方法，用于在内存受限的移动机器人上稳定运行大模型与稠密地图的联合系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在边缘机器人上同时部署大型AI模型和稠密地图面临内存不足的问题，导致关键帧无法及时加载，影响定位稳定性与模型性能。

Method: 将关键帧与室内语义区域（如房间、走廊）关联，以语义区域为单位进行地图管理，优先保留空间上相关的地图内容，减少关键帧频繁加载卸载和内存占用。

Result: 在大规模仿真环境和NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano平台上验证，相比几何方法，吞吐量提升3.3 tokens/s，延迟降低21.7%，且避免了内存溢出导致的执行中断问题。

Conclusion: 该方法有效提升了内存受限下AI集成移动机器人对稠密地图的利用效率，保障了定位稳定性和视觉语言模型的鲁棒运行。
